_mark_job(session, job, status=\"complete\")\n        return True\n\n    issued_row = ProjectVoteGroupResult.issue_group_result(\n        session=session,\n        participant=participant,\n fac_map = _item_map(factors)\n    group_type = str(group.get(\"group_type\") or \"alternative\")\n    item_lookup = fac_map if group_type == \"criteria\" else alt_map\n    criterion = group.get(\"criterion\") if isinstance(group.get(\"criterion\"), dict) else None\n    if criterion is None and group.get(\"criterion_id\") is not None:\n        criterion = answers[-1]\n        last_pair = (last.item_a, last.item_b)\n    state = SchedulerState(\n        items=items,\n        target=ranking_target_from_group(group),\n        pass_index=int(row.pass_index or 1),\n        question_budget=budget,\n        answers=list(answers),\n        prior_answers=prior,\n        last_pair=last_pair,\n    )\n\n    tick_limit = ai_pairs_per_tick()\n    pair_cap = = choose_orientation(\n            nxt[0],\n            nxt[1],\n            history,\n            [int(row.pass_index or 1), budget, *items],\n        )\n        left_item = item_lookup.get(left) or {\"title\": str(left), \"description\": \"\"}\n        right_item = item_lookup.get(right) or {\"title\": str(right), \"description\": \"\"}\n        group_size = int(budget or len(items) or 0)\n        threshold = ai_vote_threshold(group_size)\n        combined = sum(1 for a in state.answers if str(getattr(a, \"response\", \"\")).lower() in (\"skipped\", \"skip\", \"tie\", \"unsure\"))\n        allow_skip_tie = combined < threshold if threshold > 0 else False\n        # When threshold is 0 (empty group) we never allow abstention\n        messages = pairwise_messages(\n            problem_title=str(getattr(project, \"project_title\", None) or getattr(project, \"project_tag\", None) or \"\"),\n            problem_description=str(getattr(project, \"project_description\", None) or {}).get(\"description\") if criterion else None,\n            comparison_question=(criterion or {}).get(\"comparison_question\") if criterion else None,\n            group_type=group_type,\n            allow_skip_tie=allow_skip_tie,\n        )\n        response_kind = \"skipped\"\n        winner_id = left\n        try:\n            raw = chat_text_sync(\n                messages=messages,\n  choice = parse_pairwise_choice(raw)\n            if choice == \"left\":\n                response_kind = \"winner\"\n                winner_id = left\n            elif choice == \"right\":\n                response_kind = \"winner\"\n                winner_id = right\n            elif choice == \"tie\":\n                response_kind = \"tie\"\n                winner_id = left\n            elif choice == \"skipped\":\n                response_kind = \"skipped\"\n                winner_id = left\n            else:\n                response_kind = \"skipped\"\n                winner_id = left\n        except AiProviderError as ex:\n            if ex.retryable:\n                ut.show_vars_semi(\n                    f\"powerchoice_watcher - AI job {job.id} retrying\",\n                    project_id=job.project_id,\n                    error=str(ex) or type(ex).__name__,\n                )\n                return True\n            response_kind = \"skipped\"\n            winner_id = left\n\n        loser_id = right if winner_id == left else left\n        ans = PairAnswer(\n            item_a=nxt[0],\n            item_b=nxt[1],\n            winner_id=winner_id if response_kind == \"winner\" else None,\n            response=response_kind,\n            presented_left_id=left,\n            presented_right_id=right,\n            decision_seconds=0.0,\n        )\n        state.answers.append(ans)\n        state.last_pair = nxt\n        job.pair_count = int(job.pair_count or 0) + 1\n        if response_kind == \"skipped\":\n            job.skip_count = int(job.skip_count or 0) + 1\n        pairings = [answer_to_pairing(a) for a in state.answers]\n        ProjectVoteGroupResult.save_group_pairings(\n            session=session,\n            participant=participant,\n            client_group_id=package_group_id({\"group_token\": row.group_token, \"client_group_id\": row.client_group_id}),\n            pairings=pairings,\n            rank_order=rank_order_from_answers(items, state.answers),\n            clear_lock=False,\n        )\n        session.add(job)\n        session.commit()\n        session.refresh(job)\n        processed += 1\n\n    if select_next_pair(state) is not None:\n        return True\n\n    skip_rate = (job.skip_count / job.pair_count) if job.pair_count else 0.0\n    if job.pair_count and skip_rate >= ai_skip_rate_abort() and not any(a.response != \"skipped\" for a in state.answers):\n        _mark_job(session, job, status=\"failed\", error=\"AI baseline is incomplete\")\n        return True\n\n    order = rank_order_from_answers(items, state.answers)\n    ProjectVoteGroupResult.create_group_result(\n        session=session,\n        participant=participant,\n        group_type=_parse_group_type(group_type),\n        criterion_id=row.criterion_id,\n        sort_algorithm=_parse_algo(str(row.sort_algorithm or \"ford_johnson\")),\n        pass_index=int(row.pass_index or 1),\n        item_ids_initial=items,\n        rank_order=order,\n        pairings=[answer_to_pairing(a) for a in state.answers],\n {"type":"step_start","timestamp":1787339838125,"sessionID":"ses_fda3f8bc8ffeysO0s9HWnTaOQM","part":{"id":"prt_025c19290001e3g1dWeEh0Ajv4","messageID":"msg_025c18485001xJ7O04dMy2T5L9","sessionID":"ses_fda3f8bc8ffeysO0s9HWnTaOQM","snapshot":"954a69db6b3a426e406b6e9f73cdb5cf3db678e4","type":"step-start"}}
{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787339857514,"sessionID":"ses_fda3f8bc8ffeysO0s9HWnTaOQM","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"write","callID":"call_01a025c19a7f7761b10d4fe40b425037","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/src/utils/ai/prompts.py","content":"from __future__ import annotations\n\nimport math\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom ..comparison_question import normalize_comparison_question\n\n# --- quota helpers -----------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef ai_vote_threshold(group_size: int) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Combined TIE+SKIP quota before prompt restricts to 1-or-2 only.\n\n    Spec: 20% of group size with minimum 1. Group size is the number of\n    items in the sort group (len(item_ids_initial)). Once quota is reached,\n    the choice line switches to \"Respond with Option 1 or Option 2.\"\n\n    Quota is per-group, combined TIE+SKIP. Threshold 0 only for n<=0\n    (return 0 so caller can disallow abstention entirely).\n    \"\"\"\n    try:\n        n = int(group_size or 0)\n    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n        n = 0\n    if n <= 0:\n        return 0\n    ratio = 0.2\n    min_q = 1\n    try:\n        from ...config.config_settings import settings as _s\n        ratio = float(getattr(_s, \"AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_RATIO\", 0.2) or 0.2)\n        min_q = int(getattr(_s, \"AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_MIN\", 1) or 1)\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n    # also try pvf/runtime overrides if present (mirrors config.py pattern)\n    try:\n        from ...pvf.bindings.pvf_invocation import runtime_settings\n        rs = runtime_settings()\n        if rs is not None:\n            if hasattr(rs, \"AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_RATIO\"):\n                ratio = float(getattr(rs, \"AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_RATIO\") or ratio)\n            if hasattr(rs, \"AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_MIN\"):\n                min_q = int(getattr(rs, \"AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_MIN\") or min_q)\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n    ratio = max(0.01, min(0.9, float(ratio)))\n    min_q = max(1, int(min_q))\n    return max(min_q, math.ceil(n * ratio))\n\n\n# alias for clarity — spec wording\ntie_skip_quota = ai_vote_threshold\n\n\ndef combined_abstentions(answers: list[Any] | None) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Count combined skip+tie (inclusive of 'unsure') in a group.\"\"\"\n    if not answers:\n        return 0\n    cnt = 0\n    for a in answers:\n        resp = getattr(a, \"response\", None) if not isinstance(a, dict) else a.get(\"response\")\n        if resp is None:\n            continue\n        r = str(resp).lower()\n        if r in (\"skipped\", \"skip\", \"tie\", \"unsure\"):\n            cnt += 1\n    return cnt\n\n\ndef quota_reached(group_size: int, abstentions: int) -> bool:\n    thresh = ai_vote_threshold(group_size)\n    if thresh <= 0:\n        return True  # no abstentions allowed for degenerate groups\n    return int(abstentions or 0) >= thresh\n\n\n# --- template resolution + safe formatting -----------------------------------\n\n_DEFAULT_CHOICE_TIE_SKIP = \"Respond with 1, 2, TIE or SKIP. Use TIE and SKIP sparingly.\"\n_DEFAULT_CHOICE_STRICT = \"Respond with Option 1 or Option 2.\"\n\n_DEFAULT_SINGLE = (\n    \"You are an agent being asked for a ranking opinion between the following two options. \"\n    \"Choose the best overall answer based on information provided and your prior knowledge.\\n\\n\"\n    \"Project Context: {project_title}\\n{project_description}\\n\\n\"\n    \"Option 1: {option1_title}\\n{option1_description}\\n\\n\"\n    \"Option 2: {option2_title}\\n{option2_description}\\n\\n{choice_line}\"\n)\n\n_DEFAULT_FACTOR_OPTION = (\n    \"You are an agent being asked for a ranking opinion between the following two options. \"\n    \"This ranking is for a specific factor or perspective. Make a selection based on the factor \"\n    \"and specific question, keeping the factor in context, based on your overall knowledge.\\n\\n\"\n    \"Project Context: {project_title}\\n{project_description}\\n\\n\"\n    \"Current Factor: {factor_title}\\n{factor_description}\\n\\n{factor_question}\\n\\n\"\n    \"Option 1: {option1_title}\\n{option1_description}\\n\\n\"\n    \"Option 2: {option2_title}\\n{option2_description}\\n\\n{choice_line}\"\n)\n\n_DEFAULT_FACTOR_RANKING = (\n    \"You are an agent being asked for a ranking the importance of two factors being used in a \"\n    \"weighted selection ranking process. Choose the more important factor based on information \"\n    \"provided and your prior knowledge.\\n\\n\"\n    \"Project Context: {project_title}\\n{project_description}\\n\\n\"\n    \"Factor 1: {factor1_title}\\nFactor 1 Question Format: {factor1_question}\\nFactor 1 description: {factor1_description}\\n\\n\"\n    \"Factor 2: {factor2_title}\\nFactor 2 Question Format: {factor2_question}\\nFactor 2 description: {factor2_description}\\n\\n{choice_line}\"\n)\n\n\ndef _get_templates() -> tuple[str, str, str, str, str]:\n    \"\"\"Return (single_tpl, factor_opt_tpl, factor_rank_tpl, choice_tie_skip, choice_strict) from settings.\"\"\"\n    single = _DEFAULT_SINGLE\n    factor_opt = _DEFAULT_FACTOR_OPTION\n    factor_rank = _DEFAULT_FACTOR_RANKING\n    tie_skip = _DEFAULT_CHOICE_TIE_SKIP\n    strict = _DEFAULT_CHOICE_STRICT\n    try:\n        from ...config.config_settings import settings as app_s\n        single = str(getattr(app_s, \"AI_PROMPT_SINGLE_OPTION_TEMPLATE\", single) or single)\n        factor_opt = str(getattr(app_s, \"AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_OPTION_TEMPLATE\", factor_opt) or factor_opt)\n        factor_rank = str(getattr(app_s, \"AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_RANKING_TEMPLATE\", factor_rank) or factor_rank)\n        tie_skip = str(getattr(app_s, \"AI_PROMPT_CHOICE_TIE_SKIP\", tie_skip) or tie_skip)\n        strict = str(getattr(app_s, \"AI_PROMPT_CHOICE_STRICT\", strict) or strict)\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n    # runtime override (covers pvf invocation startup values if injected)\n    try:\n        from ...pvf.bindings.pvf_invocation import runtime_settings\n        rs = runtime_settings()\n        if rs is not None:\n            if hasattr(rs, \"AI_PROMPT_SINGLE_OPTION_TEMPLATE\"):\n                v = getattr(rs, \"AI_PROMPT_SINGLE_OPTION_TEMPLATE\")\n                if v:\n                    single = str(v)\n            if hasattr(rs, \"AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_OPTION_TEMPLATE\"):\n                v = getattr(rs, \"AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_OPTION_TEMPLATE\")\n                if v:\n                    factor_opt = str(v)\n            if hasattr(rs, \"AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_RANKING_TEMPLATE\"):\n                v = getattr(rs, \"AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_RANKING_TEMPLATE\")\n                if v:\n                    factor_rank = str(v)\n            if hasattr(rs, \"AI_PROMPT_CHOICE_TIE_SKIP\"):\n                v = getattr(rs, \"AI_PROMPT_CHOICE_TIE_SKIP\")\n                if v:\n                    tie_skip = str(v)\n            if hasattr(rs, \"AI_PROMPT_CHOICE_STRICT\"):\n                v = getattr(rs, \"AI_PROMPT_CHOICE_STRICT\")\n                if v:\n                    strict = str(v)\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n    return single, factor_opt, factor_rank, tie_skip, strict\n\n\ndef _safe_format(template: str, params: dict[str, Any], fallback: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Format template with params; on failure log and return fallback formatted with same params.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        return template.format(**params)\n    except Exception as ex:\n        try:\n            from ...pvf.utils import utils_show as ut\n            ut.show_vars_semi(\n                \"ai prompt format failed; using fallback\",\n                error=str(ex) or type(ex).__name__,\n                template_preview=str(template)[:300],\n                missing_keys=str(sorted(params.keys()))[:400],\n            )\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n        try:\n            return fallback.format(**params)\n        except Exception:\n            # ultimate minimal fallback\n            try:\n                from ...pvf.utils import utils_show as ut2\n                ut2.show_vars_semi(\"ai prompt fallback also failed\", error=str(ex)[:200])\n            except Exception:\n                pass\n            # return template raw with best effort\n            return fallback\n\n\ndef factor_suggest_messages(*, title: str, description: str) -> list[dict[str, str]]:\n    problem_title = (title or \"\").strip()\n    problem_description = (description or \"\").strip()\n    system = (\n        \"You suggest comparison factors for a decision problem. \"\n        \"Return JSON only, no markdown. \"\n        \"Schema: {\\\"factors\\\": [{\\\"title\\\": string, \\\"description\\\": string}]}. \"\n        \"Produce 5 to 8 distinct factors. Titles are short (2-6 words). \"\n        \"Descriptions are one or two sentences. Do not include polarity, scores, or rankings.\"\n    )\n    user = (\n        f\"Problem title: {problem_title}\\n\"\n        f\"Problem description: {problem_description}\\n\"\n        \"Suggest the factors that would most help people compare options.\"\n    )\n    return [\n        {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": system},\n        {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": user},\n    ]\n\n\ndef pairwise_messages(\n    *,\n    problem_title: str,\n    problem_description: str,\n    left_title: str,\n    left_description: str | None,\n    right_title: str,\n    right_description: str | None,\n    factor_title: str | None,\n    factor_description: str | None,\n    group_type: str,\n    comparison_question: str | None = None,\n    allow_skip_tie: bool = True,\n    # optional explicit ambient for factor-ranking (when caller has them)\n    left_factor_question: str | None = None,\n    right_factor_question: str | None = None,\n    left_factor_description: str | None = None,\n    right_factor_description: str | None = None,\n) -> list[dict[str, str]]:\n    \"\"\"Build LLM chat messages for one pairwise vote.\n\n    Randomization is handled by the caller (worker.choose_orientation) which\n    decides which stable item maps to Option 1 (left) vs Option 2 (right).\n    This function always labels the randomized presentation as Option 1 then\n    Option 2, and the caller maps \"1\"->left / \"2\"->right back to stable IDs.\n\n    Ambient placeholders are explicit (see config_settings.py):\n      single: project_title, project_description, option1_title, option1_description,\n              option2_title, option2_description, choice_line\n      factor-option: + factor_title, factor_description, factor_question\n      factor-ranking: project_title, project_description, factor1_title, factor1_question,\n                      factor1_description, factor2_title, factor2_question, factor2_description\n    \"\"\"\n    question = normalize_comparison_question(comparison_question)\n    single_tpl, factor_opt_tpl, factor_rank_tpl, tie_skip_line, strict_line = _get_templates()\n    choice_line = tie_skip_line if allow_skip_tie else strict_line\n\n    is_criteria = str(group_type).strip().lower() in (\"criteria\", \"factor\", \"factors\")\n\n    # ---- criteria / factor-importance ranking ---------------------------------\n    if is_criteria:\n        # left/right are factors being ranked. Use factor1_* / factor2_* .\n        # Caller passes left_title/right_title as factor titles; factor_description/question may be in extra args\n        # or left_description/right_description.\n        f1_title = str(left_title or \"\").strip()\n        f2_title = str(right_title or \"\").strip()\n        f1_desc = str(left_factor_description if left_factor_description is not None else (left_description or \"\")).strip()\n        f2_desc = str(right_factor_description if right_factor_description is not None else (right_description or \"\")).strip()\n        f1_q = normalize_comparison_question(left_factor_question if left_factor_question is not None else None) or \"\"\n        f2_q = normalize_comparison_question(right_factor_question if right_factor_question is not None else None) or \"\"\n        # Fallback: if no per-factor questions supplied, try factor_title's question (not applicable here)\n        params = {\n            \"project_title\": str(problem_title or \"\").strip(),\n            \"project_description\": str(problem_description or \"\").strip(),\n            \"factor1_title\": f1_title,\n            \"factor1_question\": f1_q,\n            \"factor1_description\": f1_desc,\n            \"factor2_title\": f2_title,\n            \"factor2_question\": f2_q,\n            \"factor2_description\": f2_desc,\n            \"choice_line\": choice_line,\n        }\n        content = _safe_format(factor_rank_tpl, params, _DEFAULT_FACTOR_RANKING.format(**params) if _DEFAULT_FACTOR_RANKING else factor_rank_tpl)\n        # keep a tiny system wrapper for provider compatibility; primary instruction lives in user content per spec\n        system = \"You are one participant in a pairwise comparison. Reply with exactly one token as instructed. Never explain.\"\n        if not allow_skip_tie:\n            system = \"You are one participant in a pairwise comparison. Reply with exactly one token: 1 or 2 (or Option 1 / Option 2). Never reply TIE or SKIP. Never explain.\"\n        return [\n            {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": system},\n            {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": content},\n        ]\n\n    # ---- factor-specific vs single option comparison --------------------------\n    if factor_title:\n        params = {\n            \"project_title\": str(problem_title or \"\").strip(),\n            \"project_description\": str(problem_description or \"\").strip(),\n            \"factor_title\": str(factor_title or \"\").strip(),\n            \"factor_description\": str(factor_description or \"\").strip(),\n            \"factor_question\": str(question or \"\").strip(),\n            \"option1_title\": str(left_title or \"\").strip(),\n            \"option1_description\": str(left_description or \"\").strip(),\n            \"option2_title\": str(right_title or \"\").strip(),\n            \"option2_description\": str(right_description or \"\").strip(),\n            \"choice_line\": choice_line,\n        }\n        fallback = _DEFAULT_FACTOR_OPTION\n        tpl = factor_opt_tpl\n        # fallback formatting uses default template directly to guarantee success\n        try:\n            content_try = tpl.format(**params)\n        except Exception as ex:\n            try:\n                from ...pvf.utils import utils_show as ut\n                ut.show_vars_semi(\"ai prompt factor-option format failed\", error=str(ex)[:300])\n            except Exception:\n                pass\n            content_try = _DEFAULT_FACTOR_OPTION.format(**params)\n        content = content_try\n        system = \"You are one participant in a pairwise comparison. Reply with exactly one token as instructed. Never explain.\"\n        if not allow_skip_tie:\n            system = \"You are one participant in a pairwise comparison. Reply with exactly one token: 1 or 2 (or Option 1 / Option 2). Never reply TIE or SKIP. Never explain.\"\n        return [\n            {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": system},\n            {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": content},\n        ]\n\n    # ---- single factor / overall option comparison ---------------------------\n    params = {\n        \"project_title\": str(problem_title or \"\").strip(),\n        \"project_description\": str(problem_description or \"\").strip(),\n        \"option1_title\": str(left_title or \"\").strip(),\n        \"option1_description\": str(left_description or \"\").strip(),\n        \"option2_title\": str(right_title or \"\").strip(),\n        \"option2_description\": str(right_description or \"\").strip(),\n        \"choice_line\": choice_line,\n    }\n    try:\n        content = single_tpl.format(**params)\n    except Exception as ex:\n        try:\n            from ...pvf.utils import utils_show as ut\n            ut.show_vars_semi(\"ai prompt single-option format failed\", error=str(ex)[:300])\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n        content = _DEFAULT_SINGLE.format(**params)\n    system = \"You are one participant in a pairwise comparison. Reply with exactly one token as instructed. Never explain.\"\n    if not allow_skip_tie:\n        system = \"You are one participant in a pairwise comparison. Reply with exactly one token: 1 or 2 (or Option 1 / Option 2). Never reply TIE or SKIP. Never explain.\"\n    return [\n        {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": system},\n        {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": content},\n    ]\n\n\ndef parse_pairwise_choice(text: str) -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"Parse LLM token to left/right/tie/skipped.\n\n    Accepts: 1 / 2 / Option 1 / Option 2 / LEFT / RIGHT / TIE / SKIP (and synonyms).\n    Returns \"left\" for Option 1, \"right\" for Option 2, \"tie\", \"skipped\", or None (unknown).\n    \"\"\"\n    raw = (text or \"\").strip()\n    if not raw:\n        return None\n    # first token plus full-text checks\n    first = raw.split()[0].strip().upper().strip(\".,:;\\\"'`\")\n    # numeric / option forms\n    if first in (\"1\", \"OPTION1\", \"OPTION_1\"):\n        return \"left\"\n    if first in (\"2\", \"OPTION2\", \"OPTION_2\"):\n        return \"right\"\n    # handle \"Option 1\" as two tokens — check lower\n    lowered = raw.strip().lower()\n    if lowered.startswith(\"option 1\") or lowered.startswith(\"option1\") or lowered == \"1\":\n        return \"left\"\n    if lowered.startswith(\"option 2\") or lowered.startswith(\"option2\") or lowered == \"2\":\n        return \"right\"\n    if first in (\"LEFT\", \"L\", \"WINNER_LEFT\", \"A\"):\n        return \"left\"\n    if first in (\"RIGHT\", \"R\", \"WINNER_RIGHT\", \"B\"):\n        return \"right\"\n    if first in (\"TIE\", \"EQUAL\", \"DRAW\"):\n        return \"tie\"\n    if first in (\"SKIP\", \"SKIPPED\", \"UNSURE\", \"PASS\", \"ABSTAIN\"):\n        return \"skipped\"\n    if lowered.startswith(\"left\"):\n        return \"left\"\n    if lowered.startswith(\"right\"):\n        return \"right\"\n    if lowered.startswith(\"tie\"):\n        return \"tie\"\n    if lowered.startswith(\"skip\") or lowered.startswith(\"unsure\") or lowered.startswith(\"abstain\") or lowered.startswith(\"pass\"):\n        return \"skipped\"\n    # bare numeric after stripping punctuation\n    if first == \"1\":\n        return \"left\"\n    if first == \"2\":\n        return \"right\"\n    return None\n\n\ndef parse_factor_suggestions(payload: Any) -> list[dict[str, str]]:\n    rows: list = []\n    if isinstance(payload, dict):\n        raw = payload.get(\"factors\") or payload.get(\"suggestions\") or []\n        rows = raw if isinstance(raw, list) else []\n    elif isinstance(payload, list):\n        rows = payload\n    out: list[dict[str, str]] = []\n    seen: set[str] = set()\n    for item in rows:\n        if not isinstance(item, dict):\n            continue\n        title = str(item.get(\"title\") or item.get(\"factor_title\") or \"\").strip()\n        description = str(item.get(\"description\") or item.get(\"factor_description\") or \"\").strip()\n        if not title:\n            continue\n        key = title.lower()\n        if key in seen:\n            continue\n        seen.add(key)\n        out.append({\"title\": title[:200], \"description\": description[:2000]})\n        if len(out) >= 8:\n            break\n    return out\n"},"output":"Wrote file successfully.","metadata":{"diagnostics":{},"filepath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/src/utils/ai/prompts.py","exists":true,"truncated":false},"title":"src/utils/ai/prompts.py","time":{"start":1787339857404,"end":1787339857501}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_01a025c19a7f7761b10d4fe40b425037"}},"id":"prt_025c19ac1001NsAzFNfp6JrHut","sessionID":"ses_fda3f8bc8ffeysO0s9HWnTaOQM","messageID":"msg_025c18485001xJ7O04dMy2T5L9"}}

{"type":"step_finish","timestamp":1787339868548,"sessionID":"ses_fda3f8bc8ffeysO0s9HWnTaOQM","part":{"id":"prt_025c2095f001pRsn7HgFlPzDM1","reason":"tool-calls","snapshot":"9ab09461d8490d0a879643af3dbe1e6457364212","messageID":"msg_025c1f7ca0017cXoGjdn68KWWh","sessionID":"ses_fda3f8bc8ffeysO0s9HWnTaOQM","type":"step-finish","tokens":{"total":113047,"input":577,"output":933,"reasoning":0,"cache":{"write":0,"read":111537}},"cost":0}}

{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787339900726,"sessionID":"ses_fda3f8bc8ffeysO0s9HWnTaOQM","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"write","callID":"call_01a025c229e17562ad9840809f6fee18","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/docs_examples/AI_PARTICIPANT_README.MD","content":"# AI Participants — Configuration, Prompts, and Watcher Queuing\n\n## Overview\n\nAI participants are synthetic voters that can cast pairwise rankings on the same\nsort-groups human participants see. They are **system-level** (not end-user\neditable), gated by `ACTIVATE_AI_AGENTS`, and produce a separate **Agents**\nbaseline that is excluded from human counts in the project report but shown as\n`ai_baseline` for comparison. AI work is queued and executed by the background\nwatcher process rather than the request thread.\n\n```\nBrowser / API  ->  CustomerProject (include_ai_agents, ai_voter_models)\n              ->  AiAgentJob  (queue per model_key)\n              ->  powerchoice_watcher.py --poll--> process_next_ai_job()\n              ->  ProjectVoteParticipant (source=ai, participant_key=ai:..., display_name=\"AI AGENT: ...\")\n              ->  ProjectVoteGroupResult / pairings / rank_order\n              ->  build_report_with_ai_baseline() splits human vs ai for the UI\n```\n\n---\n\n## Configuration — system level, not end-user editable\n\nAll knobs live in **`src/config/config_settings.py`** (`GlobalSettings`) and are\nread via `pvf.bindings.pvf_invocation.runtime_settings()` (startup YAML\n`ACTIVATE_AI_AGENTS` + `.env`). End users cannot edit prompts; admins configure\nproviders at the customer level (`/ws/core/customer-ai-settings`) which is\nencrypted (`enc:v1:`) and never echoed to the browser context.\n\n| Setting | Env | Default | Notes |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `ACTIVATE_AI_AGENTS` | startup YAML (`src/pvf_app_startup.yaml`) | `False` | Gate; pvf ClassVar |\n| `AI_SERVICE` | `AI_SERVICE` | `opencode_go` | `opencode_go | opencode_zen | openrouter` |\n| `AI_API_KEY` | `AI_API_KEY` | `\"\"` | System fallback; customer key preferred |\n| `AI_MODEL` | `AI_MODEL` | `glm-5.2` | Default model key |\n| `AI_MAX_CONCURRENT_PROJECTS` | `AI_MAX_CONCURRENT_PROJECTS` | `2` | Job claim concurrency |\n| `AI_MAX_PAIRS_PER_PROJECT` | `AI_MAX_PAIRS_PER_PROJECT` | `500` | Per-project pair cap |\n| `AI_PAIRS_PER_TICK` | `AI_PAIRS_PER_TICK` | `20` | Pairs processed per watcher tick |\n| `AI_SKIP_RATE_ABORT` | `AI_SKIP_RATE_ABORT` | `0.25` | Abort group if skip-rate >= threshold |\n| `AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_RATIO` | `AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_RATIO` | `0.2` | Combined TIE+SKIP quota = `ceil(group_size * ratio)` |\n| `AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_MIN` | `AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_MIN` | `1` | Minimum quota, even for small groups |\n| `AI_PROMPT_SINGLE_OPTION_TEMPLATE` | `AI_PROMPT_SINGLE_OPTION_TEMPLATE` | see below | System prompt for overall option comparison |\n| `AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_OPTION_TEMPLATE` | `AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_OPTION_TEMPLATE` | see below | Prompt when a specific factor/criterion scopes the option comparison |\n| `AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_RANKING_TEMPLATE` | `AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_RANKING_TEMPLATE` | see below | Prompt for factor-importance ranking (criteria group) |\n| `AI_PROMPT_CHOICE_TIE_SKIP` | `AI_PROMPT_CHOICE_TIE_SKIP` | `Respond with 1, 2, TIE or SKIP. Use TIE and SKIP sparingly.` | Choice line while quota remains |\n| `AI_PROMPT_CHOICE_STRICT` | `AI_PROMPT_CHOICE_STRICT` | `Respond with Option 1 or Option 2.` | Choice line once quota is reached |\n\nResolution order for every setting: explicit arg → `runtime_settings()` →\n`src/config/config_settings.py` (`app_settings`) → `src/pvf/config/pvf_config_settings.py`\n(`pvf_settings`). Helpers in **`src/utils/ai/config.py:16`** (`_runtime_settings`,\n`_app_settings`, `_pvf_settings`, `_setting`) implement this.\n\nCustomer-level overrides (encrypted at rest) are resolved by\n**`src/utils/ai/config.py:183`** `resolve_llm_credentials()` which prefers\n`customer.ai_provider` + `decrypted_ai_api_key()` (`src/pvf/utils/field_encryption.py`)\nover the system provider, and normalizes `model_key` (`__default__`).\n\nPreference locations:\n- Framework settings base: `src/pvf/config/pvf_config_settings.py:106` / `294`\n- App settings + prompt templates: `src/config/config_settings.py:11`\n- AI config helpers: `src/utils/ai/config.py:1`\n- Identity / display names: `src/utils/ai/identity.py:7`\n\n---\n\n## Prompt templates — configurable, format-string based\n\nTemplates are Python `str.format` templates stored in `config_settings.py` and read\nby **`src/utils/ai/prompts.py:72`** `_get_templates()`. They are **not** exposed\nto project owners. Editing requires a redeploy (or env override); `format`\nfailures are caught and logged via `utils_show`, falling back to built-in\ndefaults (`_DEFAULT_SINGLE`, `_DEFAULT_FACTOR_OPTION`, `_DEFAULT_FACTOR_RANKING`).\n\n### Ambient parameters (explicit)\n\n*Single option comparison* (`AI_PROMPT_SINGLE_OPTION_TEMPLATE`):\n`{project_title}` `{project_description}` `{option1_title}` `{option1_description}`\n`{option2_title}` `{option2_description}` `{choice_line}`\n\n*Factor-specific option comparison* (`AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_OPTION_TEMPLATE`):\n`{project_title}` `{project_description}` `{factor_title}` `{factor_description}`\n`{factor_question}` `{option1_title}` `{option1_description}` `{option2_title}`\n`{option2_description}` `{choice_line}`\n\n*Factor-importance ranking* (`AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_RANKING_TEMPLATE`):\n`{project_title}` `{project_description}` `{factor1_title}` `{factor1_question}`\n`{factor1_description}` `{factor2_title}` `{factor2_question}` `{factor2_description}`\n`{choice_line}`\n\nAll templates receive `{choice_line}` which is swapped per quota state (see next\nsection). `comparison_question` is normalized by\n`src/utils/comparison_question.py:4`. Placeholders are documented in\n`src/config/config_settings.py` comments and in\n`src/utils/ai/prompts.py:124` `pairwise_messages` docstring.\n\n### Default prompts\n\n**Single factor / overall option (no factor):**\n```\nYou are an agent being asked for a ranking opinion between the following two options. Choose the best overall answer based on information provided and your prior knowledge.\n\nProject Context: {project_title}\n{project_description}\n\nOption 1: {option1_title}\n{option1_description}\n\nOption 2: {option2_title}\n{option2_description}\n\n{choice_line}\n```\n\n**Multiple factors — probe of option with specific factor:**\n```\nYou are an agent being asked for a ranking opinion between the following two options. This ranking is for a specific factor or perspective. Make a selection based on the factor and specific question, keeping the factor in context, based on your overall knowledge.\n\nProject Context: {project_title}\n{project_description}\n\nCurrent Factor: {factor_title}\n{factor_description}\n\n{factor_question}\n\nOption 1: {option1_title}\n{option1_description}\n\nOption 2: {option2_title}\n{option2_description}\n\n{choice_line}\n```\n\n**Factor-importance ranking (criteria group):**\n```\nYou are an agent being asked for a ranking the importance of two factors being used in a weighted selection ranking process. Choose the more important factor based on information provided and your prior knowledge.\n\nProject Context: {project_title}\n{project_description}\n\nFactor 1: {factor1_title}\nFactor 1 Question Format: {factor1_question}\nFactor 1 description: {factor1_description}\n\nFactor 2: {factor2_title}\nFactor 2 Question Format: {factor2_question}\nFactor 2 description: {factor2_description}\n\n{choice_line}\n```\n\nChoice lines:\n- While quota remains: `Respond with 1, 2, TIE or SKIP. Use TIE and SKIP sparingly.`\n- Once quota reached: `Respond with Option 1 or Option 2.`\n\nSystem messages are intentionally minimal (\"Reply with exactly one token … Never\nexplain.\") so the templated user content carries the domain context. Error\nhandling in `_safe_format` (`src/utils/ai/prompts.py:93`) logs `ai prompt format\nfailed; using fallback` via `utils_show` and never crashes the watcher tick.\n\nParsing (`src/utils/ai/prompts.py:280` `parse_pairwise_choice`) accepts\n`1` / `2` / `Option 1` / `Option 2` / `LEFT` / `RIGHT` / `TIE` / `SKIP` (and\nsynonyms `EQUAL/DRAW`, `UNSURE/PASS/ABSTAIN`, `A/B`) and maps `1→left (Option 1)`,\n`2→right (Option 2)`. Legacy `LEFT/RIGHT` is kept for backward compatibility.\n\n---\n\n## Randomization vs. 1/2 labeling\n\nOptions (or factors in a criteria group) are randomized per pairing but always\npresented as **Option 1 then Option 2**. The response token `1` means \"the item\nshown as Option 1\" and `2` means \"Option 2\". Stability of results is preserved\nby mapping the randomized presentation back to stable IDs.\n\n- Scheduler picks canonical pair `nxt = (a,b)` via `src/utils/pair_scheduler.py:261`\n  `select_next_pair`.\n- `choose_orientation(a,b, history, seed_parts)` (`pair_scheduler.py:301`)\n  randomizes orientation deterministically (left-presentation balance + seeded\n  50/50) → `(left, right)` where `left` is shown as **Option 1**.\n- `pairwise_messages` labels `left` as Option 1 and `right` as Option 2.\n- `parse_pairwise_choice` maps `left(1) / right(2)` → `winner_id` =\n  `left` or `right`; the stored `PairAnswer` (`pair_scheduler.py:14`) retains\n  both canonical `(item_a,item_b)` and presentation `(presented_left_id,\n  presented_right_id)`, so `answer_to_pairing` / `pairings_to_answers` round-trip\n  without losing orientation. The worker uses this to build `pairings` with\n  `winner_id/loser_id` + `presented_left_id/presented_right_id` for the\n  BT/Copeland ranking.\n\n---\n\n## TIE / SKIP quota (combined, per group)\n\n- Quota per **group** (one sort group in `ProjectVoteGroupResult`) over the\n  answers accumulated so far in that group (`SchedulerState.answers`).\n- Size = `len(item_ids_initial)` (number of items in the group).\n- Quota = `max(AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_MIN, ceil(group_size * AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_RATIO))`.\n  Default ratio `0.2` with min `1` → a 6-item group allows 2 TIE/SKIP, a 2-item\n  group allows 1.\n- Implementation: `src/utils/ai/prompts.py:9` `ai_vote_threshold(group_size)` /\n  `tie_skip_quota` + `combined_abstentions` + `quota_reached`; the worker\n  (`src/utils/ai/worker.py:260`) recomputes `combined = combined_abstentions(state.answers)`\n  before each LLM call and sets `allow_skip_tie = combined < threshold`. When\n  `threshold==0` (degenerate), abstention is never allowed.\n- Prompt switch: `allow_skip_tie=True` → choice line with `TIE or SKIP`;\n  `False` → strict `Respond with Option 1 or Option 2.` (both `prompts.py` and\n  the system wrapper change). If the model still returns `TIE`/`SKIP` after the\n  quota is exhausted, it is recorded as `tie`/`skipped` and increments the\n  counter (logged via `utils_show`); the next iteration will remain strict, so\n  additional abstentions are rate-limited but not silently coerced to a winner.\n  Treat this as \"quota is a prompt gate, not a hard reject\" — SKIP is\n  **not** valid once the prompt has switched, and callers should expect rate\n  limiting rather than re-queueing.\n- Global skip guard: `AI_SKIP_RATE_ABORT` (`src/utils/ai/config.py:122`) aborts\n  a job if overall `skip_count/pair_count` ≥ `0.25` and all answers are skips\n  (see `worker.py:191`, `worker.py:356`).\n\n---\n\n## Queuing and watcher flow\n\n### Enqueue\n\n- UI toggles `CustomerProject.include_ai_agents` + `ai_voter_models` (deduped via\n  `unique_voter_models`, default key `__default__`) in\n  `src/tests/test_ai_agents.py:258` / `src/api` customer-project create/update.\n- User action `POST /ws/project-votes/ai-baseline-run` (gated by\n  `ai_features_enabled` and `include_ai_agents`) enqueues one\n  `AiAgentJob` per distinct `model_key` via `AiAgentJob.enqueue_baseline(...)`\n  (`src/db/models/ai_agent_jobs.py`). Dedupe prevents double-queue.\n\n### Storage\n\n- `AiAgentJob` row: `customer_id`, `project_id`, `model_key`, `status`\n  (`queued→in_progress→complete/failed`), `participant_id`, `pair_count`,\n  `skip_count`, `group_count`, `error_summary`. Claim is via\n  `AiAgentJob.claim_next(session, max_concurrent)` (ordered by `modify_date`\n  for round-robin fairness; see `src/tests/test_ai_agents.py:602`).\n\n### Watcher process\n\n- Entrypoint: `src/powerchoice_watcher.py:1`. Separate OS process (supervisord\n  in prod; **not** `start-server.sh`). It loads startup config\n  (`src/pvf/bindings/pvf_startup_config.py`) and registers hooks via\n  `src/app_shell.py:register_app_hooks`.\n- Loop: `process_watch_loop` (`powerchoice_watcher.py:57`) cycles\n  `WATCHER_PROCESS_MODULE_LIST` with exponential back-off\n  (`WATCHER_SHORTER_BUSY_POLL_SLEEP`, `WATCHER_LONGER_IDLE_INCREASING_POLL_SLEEP`,\n  `WATCHER_POLL_EXPONENTIAL_FACTOR`, `WATCHER_POLL_MAXIMUM_SLEEP_TIME`).\n- On the AI tag (`settings.WATCHER_PROCESS_AI_AGENTS_TAG`), each iteration\n  calls `process_next_ai_job(session)` (`src/utils/ai/worker.py:105`). If it\n  returns `True`, idle counter resets and the loopBusy-waits; else it backs off.\n- `process_next_ai_job` claims one job (`ai_max_concurrent_projects`) and runs\n  `_run_job`. Retries on `AiProviderError(retryable=True)` by returning `True`\n  without advancing counts (`worker.py:313`). Non-retryable errors become\n  `skipped` responses.\n\n### Per-job execution (`_run_job`)\n\n1. Load `CustomerProject` + alternatives/factors (`CustomerProjectAlternatives`,\n   `CustomerProjectFactors`) and resolve LLM credentials (`resolve_llm_credentials`).\n2. Get-or-create AI participant (`ProjectVoteParticipant.get_or_create_ai_participant`,\n   `source=ai`, `participant_key=ai:default` etc. via `src/utils/ai/identity.py`).\n3. Pick next sort group via `pick_next_sort_group` (`src/utils/vote_sort_session.py`).\n4. If no group / session complete → mark complete or fail on skip-rate.\n5. Issue `ProjectVoteGroupResult.issue_group_result` and build `SchedulerState`\n   from `prior_answers` + `answers`.\n6. **Tick loop** (up to `ai_pairs_per_tick`, `ai_max_pairs_per_project` cap):\n   `select_next_pair` → `choose_orientation` (randomize) → `pairwise_messages`\n   (quota-aware) → `chat_text_sync` (provider: `src/utils/ai/provider.py`) →\n   `parse_pairwise_choice` → `PairAnswer` (keep stable + presented IDs) →\n   `save_group_pairings`. Repeat until `select_next_pair` is `None`.\n7. Build final order `rank_order_from_answers` → `create_group_result` →\n   mark `group_count` and return `True` to let the watcher re-claim the same job\n   for its next group. When no groups remain, job goes `complete`.\n\nReport partitioning: `src/utils/ai/identity.py:61` `partition_vote_data` and\n`src/utils/ai/report.py:build_report_with_ai_baseline` keep AI participants\nout of `unique_participants` and emit `ai_baseline` (`available`, `option_ranking`,\n`factor_rankings`) for the UI. Providers are verified by\n`src/pvf/utils/llm_client.py` (`verify_llm_provider`).\n\n---\n\n## Files\n\n- `src/config/config_settings.py:11` — app settings + prompt templates + quota knobs\n- `src/utils/ai/config.py:1` — `ai_features_enabled`, credential resolution\n- `src/utils/ai/prompts.py:1` — templates, `ai_vote_threshold`, `combined_abstentions`, `pairwise_messages`, `parse_pairwise_choice`\n- `src/utils/ai/worker.py:1` — `process_next_ai_job`, `_run_job`, scheduler integration, randomization + quota wiring\n- `src/utils/ai/provider.py` — `chat_text_sync` / `chat_json_sync` (opencode_go / openrouter)\n- `src/utils/ai/identity.py:7` — AI keys/display names + report partitioning\n- `src/utils/pair_scheduler.py:14` — `PairAnswer`, `SchedulerState`, `select_next_pair`, `choose_orientation`, `rank_order_from_answers`\n- `src/utils/comparison_question.py:4` — question normalization\n- `src/db/models/ai_agent_jobs.py` — job queue model\n- `src/powerchoice_watcher.py:57` — `process_watch_loop` + AI tag polling\n- `src/pvf/utils/callback_delivery.py` — sibling watcher machinery (API callbacks)\n"},"output":"Wrote file successfully.","metadata":{"diagnostics":{},"filepath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/docs_examples/AI_PARTICIPANT_README.MD","exists":false,"truncated":false},"title":"docs_examples/AI_PARTICIPANT_README.MD","time":{"start":1787339900639,"end":1787339900714}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_01a025c229e17562ad9840809f6fee18"}},"id":"prt_025c22a1c0014ZAZvWqghx7Jhd","sessionID":"ses_fda3f8bc8ffeysO0s9HWnTaOQM","messageID":"msg_025c20a93001GOIp75v1SIH2IQ"}}

complete. Commit `6de6897` — `ai-agent-voting: fix SKIP/TIE threshold and decisive prompting`\\n\\n**Summary:**\\n- Audited threshold logic: prior code had no prompt-mutation for SKIP/TIE and parsed SKIP as generic failure → 100% skipped. Fixed combined counting as `ceil(group_size*0.15)` and joint removal of both options.\\n- Refined prompt decisiveness to bias away from SKIP while preserving abstention below threshold.\\n\\n**Major touch points:**\\n- `src/utils/ai/prompts.py:1` — added `import math`\\n- `src/utils/ai/prompts.py:6-37` — new helpers `ai_vote_threshold(group_size)` (`math.ceil(n*0.15)`) and `combined_abstentions(answers)` counting `skipped/skip/tie/unsure`\\n- `src/utils/ai/prompts.py:39-117` — extended `pairwise_messages(..., allow_skip_tie=True)` with branching: `allow True` → `LEFT, RIGHT, TIE or SKIP` + decisive line `Based on information about the project, make the best recommended choice for the user` + `Use TIE and SKIP sparingly...`; `allow False` → `LEFT or RIGHT` + `Never reply TIE or SKIP. Choose the better option even if imperfect.` and `Answer LEFT or RIGHT.` vs `Answer LEFT, RIGHT, TIE or SKIP.`\\n- `src/utils/ai/prompts.py:119-157` — `parse_pairwise_choice` now explicitly maps `SKIP/SKIPPED/UNSURE/PASS/ABSTAIN` → `skipped` (token and `startswith` checks)\\n- `src/utils/ai/worker.py:1` — added `import math`\\n- `src/utils/ai/worker.py:48` — import `ai_vote_threshold`\\n- `src/utils/ai/worker.py:268-285` — per-pair threshold wiring: `group_size = budget or len(items)`, `threshold = ai_vote_threshold(group_size)`, `combined = count skipped/tie/unsure in state.answers`, `allow_skip_tie = combined < threshold`, passed to `pairwise_messages`\\n- `src/utils/ai/worker.py:304-308` — explicit `elif choice == \\\"skipped\\\"` branch\\n- `src/config/config_settings.py:13` — bump `VERSION 0.7.70 → 0.7.71`\\n\\n**Verification:**\\n- Unit reproduction: `ceil(n*0.15)` correct for n=1..20; prompt snapshot shows `SKIP` present only when `allow=True` and decisive line always present; `parse_pairwise_choice(\\\"SKIP\\\") → skipped`; simulated worker loop: budget 7 threshold 2 allows 0,1 then blocks ≥2; small group n=4 threshold 1 blocks after 1 abstention.\\n- `pytest src/tests/test_comparison_question.py` — 2 passed\\n- `pytest src/tests/test_ai_agents.py` — 27 passed (including `test_all_skipped_group_fails_job`, `test_retryable_llm_error_does_not_skip`)\\n\",\"time\":{\"start\":1787339618958,\"end\":1787339622038},\"metadata\":{\"openai\":{\"itemId\":\"msg_01a025be39917cd1975863d9c8a83bc2\"}}}}\n/workspaces/powerchoice/toteboard/todo_items/item-details/ai-agent-voting-15jxk3/2026-08-21T19-08-44-692Z-build-v2l4v5.response.md:9:- `src/utils/ai/prompts.py:6-37` — new helpers `ai_vote_threshold(group_size)` (`math.ceil(n*0.15)`) and `combined_abstentions(answers)` counting `skipped/skip/tie/unsure`\n/workspaces/powerchoice/toteboard/todo_items/item-details/ai-agent-voting-15jxk3/2026-08-21T19-08-44-692Z-build-v2l4v5.response.md:13:- `src/utils/ai/worker.py:48` — import `ai_vote_threshold`\n/workspaces/powerchoice/toteboard/todo_items/item-details/ai-agent-voting-15jxk3/2026-08-21T19-08-44-692Z-build-v2l4v5.response.md:14:- `src/utils/ai/worker.py:268-285` — per-pair threshold wiring: `group_size = budget or len(items)`, `threshold = ai_vote_threshold(group_size)`, `combined = count skipped/tie/unsure in state.answers`, `allow_skip_tie = combined < threshold`, passed to `pairwise_messages`\n/workspaces/powerchoice/toteboard/todo_items/item-details/ai-agent-voting-15jxk3/2026-08-21T19-08-44-692Z-build-v2l4v5.request.md:45:- Add helper `ai_vote_threshold(group_size:int) -> int` = `math.ceil(group_size*0.15)` (import `math`). Keep minimum 1 implicitly via `ceil` except 0. Numbered Implementation Steps\\n\\n**Step 1 — Audit current threshold + prompt code (no guessing).**\\n- `src/utils/ai/prompts.py:29-83` `pairwise_messages()` — currently hard-codes `LEFT/RIGHT/TIE`, no `SKIP` mention in user/system message, no threshold param, no conditional stripping. Verify against history: `toteboard/todo_items/tree.json:233` specifies prior spec was 20% combined with `min(1)`, now required ~15% `ceil(group_size*0.15)`. Confirm absent.\\n- `src/utils/ai/worker.py:250-324` — loop `while processed < tick_limit` calls `pairwise_messages` per pair with `temperature=0.1, max_tokens=16` (`src/utils/ai/worker.py:283`). No per-pair budget/threshold check; `response_kind` defaults to `skipped` on `parse_pairwise_choice==None` (`src/utils/ai/prompts.py:86-102` only parses LEFT/RIGHT/TIE variants, so “SKIP” or “1/2” → `None` → `skipped`).\\n- `src/utils/ai/config.py:123-128` `ai_skip_rate_abort()` = 0.25 — this is *post-hoc* abort, not prompt-mutation threshold. Confirm it is unrelated to `ceil(0.15*group_size)`.\\n- `src/utils/ai/worker.py:233` `budget = requested_pairing_count or question_budget` — identify canonical `group_size` source. Compare `row.item_ids_initial`, `requested_pairing_count`, `question_budget` from `pick_next_sort_group` (`src/utils/vote_sort_session.py`) vs `src/utils/project_group_size.py:41`. Log definition.\\n\\n**Step 2 — Reproduce bug without LLM (unit).**\\n- Write `src/tests/test_ai_prompts_threshold.py` (or inline reproduction script, not committed as lifecyle artifact): snapshot `pairwise_messages(...).` assertions:\\n  - `group_size=7 → ceil(7*0.15)=2`, combined `skip+tie <2` keeps options, `>=2` removes.\\n  - Small group `n=1..6 → ceil()=1` — verify `SKIP/TIE` removed after 1 combined vote (edge per Expanded).\\n  - Zero/history edge: `group_size=0` → threshold 0 → no removal?\\n- Mock `chat_text_sync` returns `\\\"SKIP\\\"` 100% and `\\\"LEFT\\\"`; confirm current `parse_pairwise_choice` maps `\\\"SKIP\\\"`→`None`→`skipped` (`src/tests/test_ai_agents.py:619-644` shows `NOPE`→all skipped→failed).\\n\\n**Step 3 — Fix threshold math.**\\n- Add helper `ai_vote_threshold(group_size:int) -> int` = `math.ceil(group_size*0.15)` (import `math`). Keep minimum 1 implicitly via `ceil` except 0. Decide `group_size = budget` (question count) vs `len(items)` — recommendation: `budget` (`requested_pairing_count`) as it is the pair count the prompt quota guards.\\n- Count combined = `sum(1 for a in [*prior_answers, *state.answers] if a.response in (\\\"skipped\\\",\\\"tie\\\",\\\"unsure\\\"))` — ensure `tie` inclusive, match `src/utils/bt_inference/observations.py:7` `SOFT_EXCLUDE`. Verify `pair_scheduler.py:9` literals include `\\\"tie\\\"` vs `\\\"skipped\\\"`.\\n\\n**Step 4 — Fix prompt template assembly / removal.**\\n- Extend `pairwise_messages(..., allow_skip_tie: bool=True, group_size:int|None=None, combined:int|None=None)`:\\n  - When `allow_skip_tie=True`: system=`Reply with exactly one token: LEFT, RIGHT, TIE or SKIP. Use TIE and SKIP sparingly. Prefer a decisive LEFT/RIGHT.` user=`Answer LEFT, RIGHT, TIE or SKIP.`\\n  - When `False` (threshold hit): system=`Reply with exactly one token: LEFT or RIGHT. Never reply TIE or SKIP.` user=`Answer LEFT or RIGHT.` + decisive nudge: `Based on information about the project, make the best recommended choice for the user. Avoid abstention unless information is truly insufficient.`\\n- Alternative location: keep template static and mutate in `worker.py` before `chat_text_sync`. Prefer param over string-replace for testability.\\n- Update `parse_pairwise_choice` to parse `SKIP` → `\\\"skipped\\\"` (currently missing) to distinguish intentional skip from parse failure; map failures to `tie`? No — keep `None`→`skipped` but log.\\n\\n**Step 5 — Wire threshold into worker loop.**\\n- In `src/utils/ai/worker.py:248-279` before each `pairwise_messages`, compute `threshold = ceil(budget*0.15)`, `combined = count_skipped_tie(state)`, `allow = combined < threshold`. Pass to `pairwise_messages`. Ensure threshold evaluated per-pair (so mid-group threshold crossing omits options for remainder of group). Log `threshold/combined/allow` via `ut.show_vars_semi`.\\n\\n**Step 6 — Refine default decisive prompt (fix 100% SKIP bias).**\\n- Add nudge line proposed in brief + stronger variant: `You are an expert advisor. Use project context and prior knowledge; choose the better option even if imperfect. Respond SKIP only if both options lack any comparable information.`\\n- Randomize option order but keep `Option 1/2` labeling already done via `choose_orientation` (`src/utils/ai/worker.py:259`). Ensure mapping back to `item_id` preserved.\\n- Keep `temperature=0.1` (low variance helps decisive) but test `0.2-0.3` if SKIP persists; keep `max_tokens=16` prevents explanation leakage.\\n\\n**Step 7 — Verification without live LLM.**\\n- Snapshot tests for prompt output pre/post threshold.\\n- Simulated job test: inject `state.answers = [tie, skipped]` with `budget=13→threshold=2` → next prompt must omit TIE/SKIP. Mock `chat_text_sync` returning `TIE` after threshold should be treated as `skipped`+ re-prompt or counted as invalid? Decide to map to vote left/right via retry or count as skipped but threshold already hit prevents further abstentions.\\n- Run `pytest src/tests/test_ai_agents.py -k \\\"test_all_skipped_group_fails_job or test_retryable\\\"` to ensure abort logic (`ai_skip_rate_abort`) still compatible (0.25 vs 0.15 prompt threshold are independent; first restricts LLM output, second aborts job).\\n\\n**Step 8 — Scope guard.**\\n- No `toteboard/` or `board storage` writes; config changes via env `AI_SKIP_RATE_ABORT` unchanged. Only `prompts.py`/`worker.py` + new/mocked tests.\\n\\n## 2. Clarifying Questions\\n\\n1. **Group size source?** Is `group_size` the `question_budget`/`requested_pairing_count` (e.g., 13) or `len(item_ids_initial)` (e.g., 8 options)? Changes threshold from 2 vs 2 anyway but diverges for small groups.\\n2. **Joint vs independent removal?** Brief says remove *both* SKIP and TIE once combined exceeds 15%. Prior spec same. Confirm not independent (e.g., SKIP 15% separate from TIE 15%). Small group `n=4 → ceil=1` means single TIE kills SKIP too — intentional?\\n3. **Per-group or per-project quota?** Counter is `job.skip_count+tie_count` lifetime vs `state.answers` current group only. Prompt spec suggests per-group (`combined value should stop around 15% of group size`). Use current group (`state.prior_answers` excluded?) or cumulative?\\n4. **Fail-open on parse error?** If model still returns `SKIP` after threshold (despite prompt removal), should worker coercively map to `LEFT`/`RIGHT` via second-chance re-prompt, or count as `skipped` and trigger abort?\\n5. **SKIP explicitness?** Should default prompt *advertise* SKIP at all below threshold, or hide it until needed (`Use TIE and SKIP sparingly` vs never mentioning SKIP to reduce 100% bias)?\\n6. **Temperature / model variance?** Early tests 100% SKIP at `0.1` suggests prompt — not temp — bias. Keep 0.1 or raise to 0.2 for decisiveness?\\n7. **Test oracle?** Snapshot prompt strings sufficient, or require deterministic mock LLM returning `SKIP` → verify prompt mutation actually prevents future SKIP?\\n\\n## 3. Options and Trade-offs with Recommendation\\n\\n**A. Threshold enforcement point:**\\n- A1: Mutate prompt only (`pairwise_messages` param). Pros: single source, easily snapshot-tested. Cons: requires worker to pass state.\\n- A2: String-replace in worker after generation. Pros: no API change. Cons: brittle, duplicates logic.\\n- *Recommendation: A1* — add `allow_skip_tie:bool` param.\\n\\n**B. Counting scope:**\\n- B1: Combined `skip+tie` per *current group* (`state.answers`). Pros: matches spec “of group size”. Cons: allows 15% abstention every group → higher lifetime skip.\\n- B2: Combined per *job* lifetime. Pros: caps total abstention. Cons: small early groups could starve later groups.\\n- *Recommendation: B1* per-group, plus keep existing job-level `ai_skip_rate_abort=0.25` as safety net (`src/utils/ai/worker.py:191`).\\n\\n**C. Small-group handling (`ceil(0.15*n)=1` for n≤6):**\\n- C1: Strict `ceil` → 1 abstention then forced choice. Pros: spec-compliant. Cons: feels harsh for n=2.\\n- C2: `max(1, ceil(...))` same as C1; or `ceil` with floor 1 only if n≥3.\\n- *Recommendation: C1 strict, document behavior; add unit test for n=2,4,7.*\\n\\n**D. Prompt decisiveness nudge:**\\n- D1: Minimal add: `Based on information about the project, make the best recommended choice for the user.` Pros: lowest risk.\\n- D2: Strong: D1 + `Avoid SKIP unless insufficient information. If uncertain, choose the better expected outcome.` + ranked-choice framing.\\n- D3: Remove SKIP from below-threshold prompt entirely, only offer LEFT/RIGHT/TIE. Pros: eliminates SKIP bias. Cons: loses abstention signal.\\n- *Recommendation: D2* — D1 is insufficient given observed 100% SKIP; add strong nudge only while `allow_skip_tie=True`, and *remove* nudge ambiguity when `allow=False` (`Never reply TIE or SKIP`). Verify via A/B snapshot + mock where `chat_text_sync` returns `SKIP`.\\n\\n**E. Parse fix:**\\n- E1: Map explicit `SKIP` → `skipped` distinct from parse failure. Pros: metrics differentiate model intent vs hallucination.\\n- E2: Keep current `None→skipped` conflation.\\n- *Recommendation: E1* — extend `parse_pairwise_choice` to recognize `SKIP/UNSURE/PASS` tokens.\\n\\n**Overall recommendation:** Implement A1+B1+C1+D2+E1 with `threshold=ceil(budget*0.15)` evaluated per-pair against `combined=len([a for a in state.answers if a.response in (\\\"tie\\\",\\\"skipped\\\")])`, stripping both options jointly, adding decisive language below threshold and forced `LEFT/RIGHT` only complete. Commit `6de6897` — `ai-agent-voting: fix SKIP/TIE threshold and decisive prompting`\\n\\n**Summary:**\\n- Audited threshold logic: prior code had no prompt-mutation for SKIP/TIE and parsed SKIP as generic failure → 100% skipped. Fixed combined counting as `ceil(group_size*0.15)` and joint removal of both options.\\n- Refined prompt decisiveness to bias away from SKIP while preserving abstention below threshold.\\n\\n**Major touch points:**\\n- `src/utils/ai/prompts.py:1` — added `import math`\\n- `src/utils/ai/prompts.py:6-37` — new helpers `ai_vote_threshold(group_size)` (`math.ceil(n*0.15)`) and `combined_abstentions(answers)` counting `skipped/skip/tie/unsure`\\n- `src/utils/ai/prompts.py:39-117` — extended `pairwise_messages(..., allow_skip_tie=True)` with branching: `allow True` → `LEFT, RIGHT, TIE or SKIP` + decisive line `Based on information about the project, make the best recommended choice for the user` + `Use TIE and SKIP sparingly...`; `allow False` → `LEFT or RIGHT` + `Never reply TIE or SKIP. Choose the better option even if imperfect.` and `Answer LEFT or RIGHT.` vs `Answer LEFT, RIGHT, TIE or SKIP.`\\n- `src/utils/ai/prompts.py:119-157` — `parse_pairwise_choice` now explicitly maps `SKIP/SKIPPED/UNSURE/PASS/ABSTAIN` → `skipped` (token and `startswith` checks)\\n- `src/utils/ai/worker.py:1` — added `import math`\\n- `src/utils/ai/worker.py:48` — import `ai_vote_threshold`\\n- `src/utils/ai/worker.py:268-285` — per-pair threshold wiring: `group_size = budget or len(items)`, `threshold = ai_vote_threshold(group_size)`, `combined = count skipped/tie/unsure in state.answers`, `allow_skip_tie = combined < threshold`, passed to `pairwise_messages`\\n- `src/utils/ai/worker.py:304-308` — explicit `elif choice == \\\"skipped\\\"` branch\\n- `src/config/config_settings.py:13` — bump `VERSION 0.7.70 → 0.7.71`\\n\\n**Verification:**\\n- Unit reproduction: `ceil(n*0.15)` correct for n=1..20; prompt snapshot shows `SKIP` present only when `allow=True` and decisive line always present; `parse_pairwise_choice(\\\"SKIP\\\") → skipped`; simulated worker loop: budget 7 threshold 2 allows 0,1 then blocks ≥2; small group n=4 threshold 1 blocks after 1 abstention.\\n- `pytest src/tests/test_comparison_question.py` — 2 passed\\n- `pytest src/tests/test_ai_agents.py` — 27 passed (including `test_all_skipped_group_fails_job`, `test_retryable_llm_error_does_not_skip`)\\n\",\"time\":{\"start\":1787339618958,\"end\":1787339622038},\"metadata\":{\"openai\":{\"itemId\":\"msg_01a025be39917cd1975863d9c8a83bc2\"}}}}\n/workspaces/powerchoice/toteboard/todo_items/item-details/ai-agent-voting-15jxk3/2026-08-21T19-08-44-692Z-build-v2l4v5.response.md:9:- `src/utils/ai/prompts.py:6-37` — new helpers `ai_vote_threshold(group_size)` (`math.ceil(n*0.15)`) and `combined_abstentions(answers)` counting `skipped/skip/tie/unsure`\n/workspaces/powerchoice/toteboard/todo_items/item-details/ai-agent-voting-15jxk3/2026-08-21T19-08-44-692Z-build-v2l4v5.response.md:13:- `src/utils/ai/worker.py:48` — import `ai_vote_threshold`\n/workspaces/powerchoice/toteboard/todo_items/item-details/ai-agent-voting-15jxk3/2026-08-21T19-08-44-692Z-build-v2l4v5.response.md:14:- `src/utils/ai/worker.py:268-285` — per-pair threshold wiring: `group_size = budget or len(items)`, `threshold = ai_vote_threshold(group_size)`, `combined = count skipped/tie/unsure in state.answers`, `allow_skip_tie = combined < threshold`, passed to `pairwise_messages`\n/workspaces/powerchoice/toteboard/todo_items/item-details/ai-agent-voting-15jxk3/2026-08-21T19-08-44-692Z-build-v2l4v5.request.md:45:- Add helper `ai_vote_threshold(group_size:int) -> int` = `math.ceil(group_size*0.15)` (import `math`). Keep minimum 1 implicitly via `ceil` except 0. Decide `group_size = budget` (question count) vs `len(items)` — recommendation: `budget` (`requested_pairing_count`) as it is the pair count the prompt quota guards.\n/workspaces/powerchoice/toteboard/todo_items/item-details/ai-agent-voting-15jxk3/2026-08-21T19-07-25-171Z-plan-7ktpqd.raw.md:47:{\"type\":\"text\",\"timestamp\":1787339323636,\"sessionID\":\"ses_fda476c10ffeFoiKFG3XOvqkXW\",\"part\":{\"id\":\"prt_025b94c63001CqeumBi2OL4ZKg\",\"messageID\":\"msg_025b932e7002S3As5RCTnPKDZw\",\"sessionID\":\"ses_fda476c10ffeFoiKFG3XOvqkXW\",\"type\":\"text\",\"text\":\"# AI Agent Voting — Execution Plan (READ-ONLY)\\n\\n## 1. Numbered Implementation Steps\\n\\n**Step 1 — Audit current threshold + prompt code (no guessing).**\\n- `src/utils/ai/prompts.py:29-83` `pairwise_messages()` — currently hard-codes `LEFT/RIGHT/TIE`, no `SKIP` mention in user/system message, no threshold param, no conditional stripping. Verify against history: `toteboard/todo_items/tree.json:233` specifies prior spec was 20% combined with `min(1)`, now required ~15% `ceil(group_size*0.15)`. Confirm absent.\\n- `src/utils/ai/worker.py:250-324` — loop `while processed < tick_limit` calls `pairwise_messages` per pair with `temperature=0.1, max_tokens=16` (`src/utils/ai/worker.py:283`). No per-pair budget/threshold check; `response_kind` defaults to `skipped` on `parse_pairwise_choice==None` (`src/utils/ai/prompts.py:86-102` only parses LEFT/RIGHT/TIE variants, so “SKIP” or “1/2” → `None` → `skipped`).\\n- `src/utils/ai/config.py:123-128` `ai_skip_rate_abort()` = 0.25 — this is *post-hoc* abort, not prompt-mutation threshold. Confirm it is unrelated to `ceil(0.15*group_size)`.\\n- `src/utils/ai/worker.py:233` `budget = requested_pairing_count or question_budget` — identify canonical `group_size` source. Compare `row.item_ids_initial`, `requested_pairing_count`, `question_budget` from `pick_next_sort_group` (`src/utils/vote_sort_session.py`) vs `src/utils/project_group_size.py:41`. Log definition.\\n\\n**Step 2 — Reproduce bug without LLM (unit).**\\n- Write `src/tests/test_ai_prompts_threshold.py` (or inline reproduction script, not committed as lifecyle artifact): snapshot `pairwise_messages(...).` assertions:\\n  - `group_size=7 → ceil(7*0.15)=2`, combined `skip+tie <2` keeps options, `>=2` removes.\\n  - Small group `n=1..6 → ceil()=1` — verify `SKIP/TIE` removed after 1 combined vote (edge per Expanded).\\n  - Zero/history edge: `group_size=0` → threshold 0 → no removal?\\n- Mock `chat_text_sync` returns `\\\"SKIP\\\"` 100% and `\\\"LEFT\\\"`; confirm current `parse_pairwise_choice` maps `\\\"SKIP\\\"`→`None`→`skipped` (`src/tests/test_ai_agents.py:619-644` shows `NOPE`→all skipped→failed).\\n\\n**Step 3 — Fix threshold math.**\\n- Add helper `ai_vote_threshold(group_size:int) -> int` = `math.ceil(group_size*0.15)` (import `math`). Keep minimum 1 implicitly via `ceil` except 0. Decide `group_size = budget` (question count) vs `len(items)` — recommendation: `budget` (`requested_pairing_count`) as it is the pair count the prompt quota guards.\\n- Count combined = `sum(1 for a in [*prior_answers, *state.answers] if a.response in (\\\"skipped\\\",\\\"tie\\\",\\\"unsure\\\"))` — ensure `tie` inclusive, match `src/utils/bt_inference/observations.py:7` `SOFT_EXCLUDE`. Verify `pair_scheduler.py:9` literals include `\\\"tie\\\"` vs `\\\"skipped\\\"`.\\n\\n**Step 4 — Fix prompt template assembly / removal.**\\n- Extend `pairwise_messages(..., allow_skip_tie: bool=True, group_size:int|None=None, combined:int|None=None)`:\\n  - When `allow_skip_tie=True`: system=`Reply with exactly one token: LEFT, RIGHT, TIE or SKIP. Use TIE and SKIP sparingly. Prefer a decisive LEFT/RIGHT.` user=`Answer LEFT, RIGHT, TIE or SKIP.`\\n  - When `False` (threshold hit): system=`Reply with exactly one token: LEFT or RIGHT. Never reply TIE or SKIP.` user=`Answer LEFT or RIGHT.` + decisive nudge: `Based on information about the project, make the best recommended choice for the user. Avoid abstention unless information is truly insufficient.`\\n- Alternative location: keep template static and mutate in `worker.py` before `chat_text_sync`. Prefer param over string-replace for testability.\\n- Update `parse_pairwise_choice` to parse `SKIP` → `\\\"skipped\\\"` (currently missing) to distinguish intentional skip from parse failure; map failures to `tie`? No — keep `None`→`skipped` but log.\\n\\n**Step 5 — Wire threshold into worker loop.**\\n- In `src/utils/ai/worker.py:248-279` before each `pairwise_messages`, compute `threshold = ceil(budget*0.15)`, `combined = count_skipped_tie(state)`, `allow = combined < threshold`. Pass to `pairwise_messages`. Ensure threshold evaluated per-pair (so mid-group threshold crossing omits options for remainder of group). Log `threshold/combined/allow` via `ut.show_vars_semi`.\\n\\n**Step 6 — Refine default decisive prompt (fix 100% SKIP bias).**\\n- Add nudge line proposed in brief + stronger variant: `You are an expert advisor. Use project context and prior knowledge; choose the better option even if imperfect. Respond SKIP only if both options lack any comparable information.`\\n- Randomize option order but keep `Option 1/2` labeling already done via `choose_orientation` (`src/utils/ai/worker.py:259`). Ensure mapping back to `item_id` preserved.\\n- Keep `temperature=0.1` (low variance helps decisive) but test `0.2-0.3` if SKIP persists; keep `max_tokens=16` prevents explanation leakage.\\n\\n**Step 7 — Verification without live LLM.**\\n- Snapshot tests for prompt output pre/post threshold.\\n- Simulated job test: inject `state.answers = [tie, skipped]` with `budget=13→threshold=2` → next prompt must omit TIE/SKIP. Mock `chat_text_sync` returning `TIE` after threshold should be treated as `skipped`+ re-prompt or counted as invalid? Decide to map to vote left/right via retry or count as skipped but threshold already hit prevents further abstentions.\\n- Run `pytest src/tests/test_ai_agents.py -k \\\"test_all_skipped_group_fails_job or test_retryable\\\"` to ensure abort logic (`ai_skip_rate_abort`) still compatible (0.25 vs 0.15 prompt threshold are independent; first restricts LLM output, second aborts job).\\n\\n**Step 8 — Scope guard.**\\n- No `toteboard/` or `board storage` writes; config changes via env `AI_SKIP_RATE_ABORT` unchanged. Only `prompts.py`/`worker.py` + new/mocked tests.\\n\\n## 2. Clarifying Questions\\n\\n1. **Group size source?** Is `group_size` the `question_budget`/`requested_pairing_count` (e.g., 13) or `len(item_ids_initial)` (e.g., 8 options)? Changes threshold from 2 vs 2 anyway but diverges for small groups.\\n2. **Joint vs independent removal?** Brief says remove *both* SKIP and TIE once combined exceeds 15%. Prior spec same. Confirm not independent (e.g., SKIP 15% separate from TIE 15%). Small group `n=4 → ceil=1` means single TIE kills SKIP too — intentional?\\n3. **Per-group or per-project quota?** Counter is `job.skip_count+tie_count` lifetime vs `state.answers` current group only. Prompt spec suggests per-group (`combined value should stop around 15% of group size`). Use current group (`state.prior_answers` excluded?) or cumulative?\\n4. **Fail-open on parse error?** If model still returns `SKIP` after threshold (despite prompt removal), should worker coercively map to `LEFT`/`RIGHT` via second-chance re-prompt, or count as `skipped` and trigger abort?\\n5. **SKIP explicitness?** Should default prompt *advertise* SKIP at all below threshold, or hide it until needed (`Use TIE and SKIP sparingly` vs never mentioning SKIP to reduce 100% bias)?\\n6. **Temperature / model variance?** Early tests 100% SKIP at `0.1` suggests prompt — not temp — bias. Keep 0.1 or raise to 0.2 for decisiveness?\\n7. **Test oracle?** Snapshot prompt strings sufficient, or require deterministic mock LLM returning `SKIP` → verify prompt mutation actually prevents future SKIP?\\n\\n## 3. Options and Trade-offs with Recommendation\\n\\n**A. Threshold enforcement point:**\\n- A1: Mutate prompt only (`pairwise_messages` param). Pros: single source, easily snapshot-tested. Cons: requires worker to pass state.\\n- A2: String-replace in worker after generation. Pros: no API change. Cons: brittle, duplicates logic.\\n- *Recommendation: A1* — add `allow_skip_tie:bool` param.\\n\\n**B. Counting scope:**\\n- B1: Combined `skip+tie` per *current group* (`state.answers`). Pros: matches spec “of group size”. Cons: allows 15% abstention every group → higher lifetime skip.\\n- B2: Combined per *job* lifetime. Pros: caps total abstention. Cons: small early groups could starve later groups.\\n- *Recommendation: B1* per-group, plus keep existing job-level `ai_skip_rate_abort=0.25` as safety net (`src/utils/ai/worker.py:191`).\\n\\n**C. Small-group handling (`ceil(0.15*n)=1` for n≤6):**\\n- C1: Strict `ceil` → 1 abstention then forced choice. Pros: spec-compliant. Cons: feels harsh for n=2.\\n- C2: `max(1, ceil(...))` same as C1; or `ceil` with floor 1 only if n≥3.\\n- *Recommendation: C1 strict, document behavior; add unit test for n=2,4,7.*\\n\\n**D. Prompt decisiveness nudge:**\\n- D1: Minimal add: `Based on information about the project, make the best recommended choice for the user.` Pros: lowest risk.\\n- D2: Strong: D1 + `Avoid SKIP unless insufficient information. If uncertain, choose the better expected outcome.` + ranked-choice framing.\\n- D3: Remove SKIP from below-threshold prompt entirely, only offer LEFT/RIGHT/TIE. Pros: eliminates SKIP bias. Cons: loses abstention signal.\\n- *Recommendation: D2* — D1 is insufficient given observed 100% SKIP; add strong nudge only while `allow_skip_tie=True`, and *remove* nudge ambiguity when `allow=False` (`Never reply TIE or SKIP`). Verify via A/B snapshot + mock where `chat_text_sync` returns `SKIP`.\\n\\n**E. Parse fix:**\\n- E1: Map explicit `SKIP` → `skipped` distinct from parse failure. Pros: metrics differentiate model intent vs hallucination.\\n- E2: Keep current `None→skipped` conflation.\\n- *Recommendation: E1* — extend `parse_pairwise_choice` to recognize `SKIP/UNSURE/PASS` tokens.\\n\\n**Overall recommendation:** Implement A1+B1+C1+D2+E1 with `threshold=ceil(budget*0.15)` evaluated per-pair against `combined=len([a for a in state.answers if a.response in (\\\"tie\\\",\\\"skipped\\\")])`, stripping both options jointly, adding decisive language below threshold and forced `LEFT/RIGHT` only above.\\n\",\"time\":{\"start\":1787339295843,\"end\":1787339323624},\"metadata\":{\"openai\":{\"itemId\":\"msg_01a025b94c1071e185fe5c3655eeb758\"}}}}\n/workspaces/powerchoice/src/utils/ai/prompts.py:10:def ai_vote_threshold(group_size: int) -> int:\n","exit":0,"truncated":true,"outputPath":"/home/ubuntu/.local/share/opencode/tool-output/tool_025c2a9cf0016BHwXFJdtO6p93"},"title":"grep -rn \"ai_vote_threshold\" /workspaces/powerchoice --include=\"*.py\" --include=\"*.md\" | head -20","time":{"start":1787339910034,"end":1787339910050}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_01a025c2a0f67a609ae1b422d03d1138"}},"id":"prt_025c2a8fc001PS0u9epfl1Gz3V","sessionID":"ses_fda3f8bc8ffeysO0s9HWnTaOQM","messageID":"msg_025c29c590010WG9cvje6DgsV9"}}
